/app/src/providers/AGENTS.md\n# Provider Implementations\n\nLLM provider integrations implementing `ApiProvider` interface to call different AI services.\n\n## Architecture\n\nEach provider:\n\n- Implements `ApiProvider` interface (`src/types/providers.ts`)\n- Transforms prompts → provider-specific API format\n- Returns normalized `ProviderResponse`\n- Handles auth, rate limits, retries, streaming\n\n## Provider Lifecycle & Cleanup\n\nThe evaluator (`src/evaluator.ts`) manages provider lifecycle. After evaluation completes, it calls `providerRegistry.shutdownAll()` to clean up resources.\n\n**If your provider allocates resources** (Python workers, connections, child processes):\n\n- Implement a `cleanup()` method on your provider\n- Register with `providerRegistry` for automatic cleanup\n- Resources are released in the evaluator's `finally` block\n\n**Reference implementations:**\n\n- `openai.ts` - Most comprehensive\n- `anthropic/index.ts` - Complex provider with subdirectory\n- `http.ts` - Generic HTTP pattern\n\n## Logging\n\nSee `docs/agents/logging.md` - use logger with object context (auto-sanitized).\n\n## Common Patterns\n\n**OpenAI-compatible providers** extend `OpenAiChatCompletionProvider`. See `src/providers/quiverai.ts` for a minimal example or `src/providers/openrouter.ts` for a more complex one.\n\n**Config priority:** Explicit options > Environment variables > Provider defaults\n\n## Caching Best Practices\n\nWhen implementing caching in your provider, **ALWAYS set the `cached: true` flag** when returning a cached response:\n\n```typescript\n// ✅ CORRECT - Always set cached flag\nif (cachedResponse) {\n  const parsed = JSON.parse(cachedResponse as string);\n  return { ...parsed, cached: true };\n}\n\n// ❌ WRONG - Missing cached flag\nif (cachedResponse) {\n  return JSON.parse(cachedResponse as string);\n}\n```\n\n**Why this matters:**\n\n- The `cached` flag allows downstream code to skip rate limiting delays\n- Performance metrics can distinguish between fresh API calls and cache hits\n- Evaluations can accurately track which responses were served from cache\n\n**Response type compatibility:**\n\n- `ProviderResponse` has `cached?: boolean`\n- `ProviderEmbeddingResponse` has `cached?: boolean`\n- `ProviderModerationResponse` has `cached?: boolean`\n\n**Reference implementations:**\n\n- `bedrock/converse.ts:985` - Sets cached flag correctly\n- `pythonCompletion.ts:194` - Example with spread operator\n- `google/vertex.ts:268` - Multiple cache points handled correctly\n\n## Testing Requirements\n\n**CRITICAL: Tests must NEVER make real API calls.** Mock all HTTP requests.\n\nEvery provider needs tests in `test/providers/`:\n\n- Mock API responses using `vi.mock`\n- Test success AND error cases\n- Test rate limits, timeouts, invalid configs\n- Run with: `npx vitest run test/providers/my-provider`\n\n## Adding a Provider\n\n**All seven items are required** before a provider is complete:\n\n1. Implement `ApiProvider` interface\n2. Add env vars to `src/types/env.ts` (`ProviderEnvOverridesSchema`)\n3. Add env vars to `src/envars.ts` (if documenting in CLI help)\n4. Add tests in `test/providers/`\n5. Add docs in `site/docs/providers/<provider>.md`\n6. Add entry to `site/docs/providers/index.md` table (alphabetical order)\n7. Add example in `examples/<provider>/`\n\nAfter updating env schema, regenerate JSON schema: `npm run jsonSchema:generate`\n\n**Verify completeness:**\n\n```bash\n# Check all pieces exist\nls 'package.json'));\n\n  // Try to find package.json using require.resolve with package.json subpath\n  // This handles monorepos, workspaces, pnpm, etc.\n  let packageJsonPath: string;\n  try {\n    packageJsonPath = require.resolve(`${packageName}/package.json`);\n  } catch {\n    // Fallback: construct direct path for simple node_modules structure\n    packageJsonPath = path.join(\n      basePath,\n      'node_modules',\n      ...packageName.split('/'),\n      'package.json',\n    );\n    if (!fs.existsSync(packageJsonPath)) {\n      throw new Error(`Cannot find ${packageName}/package.json`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const packageDir = path.dirname(packageJsonPath);\n  const packageJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(packageJsonPath, 'utf-8'));\n\n  // Extract ESM entry point from exports field\n  let esmEntry: string | undefined;\n\n  if (packageJson.exports) {\n    const mainExport =\n      packageJson.exports[exportPath] ||\n      (exportPath === '.' ? packageJson.exports['.'] || packageJson.exports : undefined);\n    if (typeof mainExport === 'string') {\n      esmEntry = mainExport;\n    } else if (typeof mainExport === 'object') {\n      // Prefer \"import\" condition for ESM\n      esmEntry = mainExport.import || mainExport.default;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Fallback to module or main field\n  if (!esmEntry) {\n    esmEntry = packageJson.module || packageJson.main;\n  }\n\n  if (!esmEntry) {\n    throw new Error(`Cannot find ESM entry point in ${packageName}/package.json`);\n  }\n\n  return path.join(packageDir, esmEntry);\n}\n\nfunction unwrapOpenCodeResult<T>(result: OpenCodeSdkResult<T> | undefined): T | undefined {\n  if (result === undefined || result === null) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  if (typeof result === 'object' && 'data' in result) {\n    return result.data as T | undefined;\n  }\n  return result as T;\n}\n\nfunction getSessionPath(sessionId: string): OpenCodeSessionPath {\n  return {\n    id: sessionId,\n    sessionID: sessionId,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction tryParseJson(value: string): string | undefined {\n  try {\n    return JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(value));\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction normalizeStructuredText(value: string): string | undefined {\n  const trimmedValue = value.trim();\n  const directJson = tryParseJson(trimmedValue);\n  if (directJson) {\n    return directJson;\n  }\n\n  const fencedJsonMatch = trimmedValue.match(/^```(?:json)?\\s*([\\s\\S]*?)\\s*```$/i);\n  if (!fencedJsonMatch?.[1]) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n\n  return tryParseJson(fencedJsonMatch[1]);\n}\n\n/**\n * Helper to load the OpenCode SDK ESM module\n *\n * Uses a two-phase approach:\n * 1. Try simple dynamic import - works when SDK is in same node_modules tree\n * 2. Fall back to smart ESM resolution for edge cases (pnpm, global installs, monorepos)\n */\nasync function loadOpenCodeSDK(): Promise<LoadedOpenCodeSDKModule> {\n  const directImports = [\n    { specifier: '@opencode-ai/sdk/v2', exportPath: './v2' as const, apiVersion: 'v2' as const },\n    { specifier: '@opencode-ai/sdk', exportPath: '.' as const, apiVersion: 'v1' as const },\n  ];\n\n  for (const candidate of directImports) {\n    try {\n      logger.debug(`Attempting dynamic import of ${candidate.specifier}`);\n      const module = (await import(candidate.specifier)) as unknown as OpenCodeSDKModule;\n      return { ...module, apiVersion: candidate.apiVersion };\n    } catch (error) {\n      logger.debug(`Dynamic import failed for ${candidate.specifier}`, { error });\n    }\n  }\n\n  const basePath =\n    cliState.basePath && path.isAbsolute(cliState.basePath) ? cliState.basePath : process.cwd();\n\n  for (const candidate of directImports) {\n    try {\n      const modulePath = resolveEsmPackage('@opencode-ai/sdk', candidate.exportPath, basePath);\n      logger.debug(`Resolved OpenCode SDK path (${candidate.apiVersion}): ${modulePath}`);\n      const module = (await importModule(modulePath)) as OpenCodeSDKModule;\n      return { ...module, apiVersion: candidate.apiVersion };\n    } catch (error) {\n      logger.debug(`Smart resolution failed for ${candidate.specifier}`, { error });\n    }\n  }\n\n  const err = new Error('Failed to resolve @opencode-ai/sdk');\n  logger.error(`Failed to load OpenCode SDK: ${err}`);\n  throw new Error(\n    dedent`The @opencode-ai/sdk package is required but not installed.\n\n    To use the OpenCode SDK provider, install it with:\n      npm install @opencode-ai/sdk\n\n    For more information, see: https://www.promptfoo.dev/docs/providers/opencode-sdk/`,\n  );\n}\n\nexport class OpenCodeSDKProvider implements ApiProvider {\n  config: OpenCodeSDKConfig;\n  env?: EnvOverrides;\n\n  private providerId = 'opencode:sdk';\n  private opencodeModule?: LoadedOpenCodeSDKModule;\n  private client?: OpenCodeClient;\n  private server?: OpenCodeServer;\n  private sessions: Map<string, OpenCodeSessionHandle> = new Map(); // cacheKey -> session info\n  private sessionOrder: string[] = []; // Track insertion order for LRU eviction\n\n  constructor(\n    options: {\n      id?: string;\n      config?: OpenCodeSDKConfig;\n      env?: EnvOverrides;\n    } = {},\n  ) {\n    const { config, env, id } = options;\n    this.config = config ?? {};\n    this.env = env;\n    this.providerId = id ?? this.providerId;\n  }\n\n  id(): string {\n    return this.providerId;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get API key based on provider_id or common environment variables\n   */\n  getApiKey(config: OpenCodeSDKConfig = this.config): string | undefined {\n    if (config?.apiKey) {\n      return config.apiKey;\n    }\n\n    // Check provider-specific env vars based on provider_id\n    const providerId = config?.provider_id?.toLowerCase();\n    if (providerId === 'anthropic') {\n      return this.env?.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY || getEnvString('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY');\n    }\n    if (providerId === 'openai') {\n      return this.env?.OPENAI_API_KEY || getEnvString('OPENAI_API_KEY');\n    }\n    if (providerId === 'google') {\n      return this.env?.GOOGLE_API_KEY || getEnvString('GOOGLE_API_KEY');\n    }\n\n    // Fall back to common env vars\n    return (\n      this.env?.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY ||\n      getEnvString('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY') ||\n      this.env?.OPENAI_API_KEY ||\n      getEnvString('OPENAI_API_KEY')\n    );\n  }\n\n  toString(): string {\n    return '[OpenCode SDK Provider]';\n  }\n\n  async cleanup(): Promise<void> {\n    for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {\n      try {\n        await this.deleteSession(session);\n      } catch (err) {\n        logger.debug(`Failed to delete persistent session ${session.id}: ${err}`);\n      }\n    }\n    this.sessions.clear();\n    this.sessionOrder = [];\n\n    // Close server if we started one\n    if (this.server) {\n      try {\n        this.server.close();\n      } catch (err) {\n        logger.debug(`Failed to close OpenCode server: ${err}`);\n      }\n      this.server = undefined;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Build the tools configuration based on config and defaults\n   */\n  private buildToolsConfig(config: OpenCodeSDKConfig): OpenCodeToolConfig | undefined {\n    // If explicit tools config provided, use it\n    if (config.tools) {\n      return config.tools;\n    }\n\n    // If no working_dir, disable all tools (chat-only mode)\n    if (!config.working_dir) {\n      return {\n        bash: false,\n        edit: false,\n        write: false,\n        read: false,\n        grep: false,\n        glob: false,\n        list: false,\n        patch: false,\n        todowrite: false,\n        todoread: false,\n        webfetch: false,\n        question: false,\n        skill: false,\n        lsp: false,\n      };\n    }\n\n    // With working_dir, enable read-only tools by default\n    return {\n      bash: false,\n      edit: false,\n      write: false,\n      read: true,\n      grep: true,\n      glob: true,\n      list: true,\n      patch: false,\n      todowrite: false,\n      todoread: false,\n      webfetch: false,\n      question: false,\n      skill: false,\n      lsp: false,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private buildQuery(\n    config: OpenCodeSDKConfig,\n    workingDir: string | undefined,\n  ): OpenCodeSessionQuery | undefined {\n    const query: OpenCodeSessionQuery = {};\n\n    if (config.working_dir && workingDir) {\n      query.directory = workingDir;\n    }\n    if (config.workspace) {\n      query.workspace = config.workspace;\n    }\n\n    return Object.keys(query).length > 0 ? query : undefined;\n  }\n\n  private buildSessionKey(config: OpenCodeSDKConfig, workingDir: string | undefined): string {\n    return generateCacheKey('opencode:sdk:session', {\n      baseUrl: config.baseUrl,\n      workingDir: config.working_dir ? workingDir : undefined,\n      workspace: config.workspace,\n      provider_id: config.provider_id,\n      model: config.model,\n      tools: this.buildToolsConfig(config),\n      permission: config.permission,\n      agent: config.agent,\n      custom_agent: config.custom_agent,\n      format: config.format,\n      variant: config.variant,\n      mcp: config.mcp,\n    });\n  }\n\n  private buildServerEnv(config: OpenCodeSDKConfig): Record<string, string> {\n    const serverEnv: Record<string, string> = {};\n\n    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(process.env)) {\n      if (value !== undefined) {\n        serverEnv[key] = value;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (this.env) {\n      for (const key of Object.keys(this.env).sort()) {\n        const value = this.env[key];\n        if (value !== undefined) {\n          serverEnv[key] = value;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (config.log_level === 'debug' || isDebugMode()) {\n      serverEnv.DEBUG = serverEnv.DEBUG || 'opencode:*';\n      logger.debug('[OpenCode SDK] Debug mode enabled, synced from promptfoo log level');\n    }\n\n    const homeDir = os.homedir();\n    const opencodeBinPath = path.join(homeDir, '.opencode', 'bin');\n    if (!serverEnv.PATH?.includes(opencodeBinPath)) {\n      serverEnv.PATH = `${opencodeBinPath}:${serverEnv.PATH ?? ''}`;\n      logger.debug(`Added ${opencodeBinPath} to PATH for OpenCode CLI`);\n    }\n\n    return serverEnv;\n  }\n\n  private buildServerConfig(config: OpenCodeSDKConfig): Record<string, unknown> {\n    const serverConfig: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n\n    if (config.log_level) {\n      serverConfig.logLevel = config.log_level;\n    }\n\n    if (config.mcp && Object.keys(config.mcp).length > 0) {\n      serverConfig.mcp = config.mcp;\n      logger.debug(`[OpenCode SDK] Configuring MCP servers: ${Object.keys(config.mcp).join(', ')}`);\n    }\n\n    if (config.custom_agent) {\n      serverConfig.agent = {\n        custom: {\n          description: config.custom_agent.description,\n          model: config.custom_agent.model,\n          temperature: config.custom_agent.temperature,\n          top_p: config.custom_agent.top_p,\n          tools: config.custom_agent.tools,\n          permission: config.custom_agent.permission,\n          prompt: config.custom_agent.prompt,\n          mode: config.custom_agent.mode ?? 'primary',\n          maxSteps: config.custom_agent.steps ?? config.custom_agent.maxSteps,\n          color: config.custom_agent.color,\n          disable: config.custom_agent.disable,\n          hidden: config.custom_agent.hidden,\n        },\n      };\n      logger.debug(`[OpenCode SDK] Configuring custom agent: ${config.custom_agent.description}`);\n    }\n\n    if (config.permission) {\n      serverConfig.permission = config.permission;\n      logger.debug('[OpenCode SDK] Configuring global permissions');\n    }\n\n    const toolsConfig = this.buildToolsConfig(config);\n    if (toolsConfig) {\n      serverConfig.tools = toolsConfig;\n    }\n\n    if (config.provider_id && config.apiKey) {\n      serverConfig.provider = {\n        [config.provider_id]: {\n          options: {\n            apiKey: config.apiKey,\n          },\n        },\n      };\n      logger.debug(`[OpenCode SDK] Injecting provider apiKey for ${config.provider_id}`);\n    }\n\n    return serverConfig;\n  }\n\n  private warnOnIgnoredBaseUrlConfig(config: OpenCodeSDKConfig): void {\n    if (!config.baseUrl) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const ignoredSettings = [\n      config.hostname === undefined ? undefined : 'hostname',\n      config.port === undefined ? undefined : 'port',\n      config.timeout === undefined ? undefined : 'timeout',\n      config.log_level === undefined ? undefined : 'log_level',\n      config.mcp ? 'mcp' : undefined,\n      config.custom_agent ? 'custom_agent' : undefined,\n      config.apiKey ? 'apiKey' : undefined,\n    ].filter(Boolean);\n\n    if (ignoredSettings.length > 0) {\n      logger.warn(\n        `[OpenCode SDK] baseUrl uses an existing OpenCode server. These config keys are ignored unless that server is preconfigured: ${ignoredSettings.join(', ')}`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  private buildDeleteSessionParameters(session: OpenCodeSessionHandle): Record<string, unknown> {\n    if (!this.opencodeModule) {\n      throw new Error('OpenCode SDK module is not loaded');\n    }\n\n    if (this.opencodeModule.apiVersion === 'v2') {\n      return {\n        sessionID: session.id,\n        ...session.query,\n      };\n    }\n\n    return {\n      path: getSessionPath(session.id),\n      query: session.query,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private async deleteSession(session: OpenCodeSessionHandle | undefined): Promise<void> {\n    if (!session || !this.client?.session?.delete) {\n      return;\n    }\n    await this.client.session.delete(this.buildDeleteSessionParameters(session));\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Add a session to the cache with LRU eviction\n   */\n  private addSession(cacheKey: string, session: OpenCodeSessionHandle): void {\n    // Remove oldest sessions if we've hit the limit\n    while (this.sessions.size >= MAX_SESSIONS && this.sessionOrder.length > 0) {\n      const oldestKey = this.sessionOrder.shift();\n      if (oldestKey) {\n        const oldSession = this.sessions.get(oldestKey);\n        this.sessions.delete(oldestKey);\n        // Best-effort cleanup of old session\n        if (oldSession) {\n          this.deleteSession(oldSession).catch((err) => {\n            logger.debug(`Failed to delete evicted session ${oldSession.id}: ${err}`);\n          });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    this.sessions.set(cacheKey, session);\n    this.sessionOrder.push(cacheKey);\n  }\n\n  private prepareCall(context?: CallApiContextParams): OpenCodePreparedCall {\n    const config: OpenCodeSDKConfig = {\n      ...this.config,\n      ...context?.prompt?.config,\n    };\n\n    if (config.workspace && !config.baseUrl && !config.working_dir) {\n      throw new Error('OpenCode SDK workspace support requires either baseUrl or working_dir');\n    }\n\n    if (config.apiKey && !config.provider_id && !config.baseUrl) {\n      logger.warn(\n        '[OpenCode SDK] apiKey is set without provider_id. Prefer setting provider_id so promptfoo can wire the credential into the spawned OpenCode server.',\n      );\n    }\n\n    this.warnOnIgnoredBaseUrlConfig(config);\n\n    if (config.working_dir) {\n      const workingDir = path.isAbsolute(config.working_dir)\n        ? config.working_dir\n        : path.resolve(process.cwd(), config.working_dir);\n\n      let stats: fs.Stats;\n      try {\n        stats = fs.statSync(workingDir);\n      } catch (err: any) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Working directory ${config.working_dir} (resolved to ${workingDir}) does not exist or isn't accessible: ${err.message}`,\n        );\n      }\n\n      if (!stats.isDirectory()) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Working directory ${config.working_dir} (resolved to ${workingDir}) is not a directory`,\n        );\n      }\n\n      return {\n        config,\n        isTempDir: false,\n        workingDir,\n      };\n    }\n\n    return {\n      config,\n      isTempDir: true,\n      workingDir: fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'promptfoo-opencode-sdk-')),\n    };\n  }\n\n  private async ensureClient(config: OpenCodeSDKConfig): Promise<void> {\n    if (!this.opencodeModule) {\n      this.opencodeModule = await loadOpenCodeSDK();\n    }\n\n    if (this.client) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const { createOpencode, createOpencodeClient } = this.opencodeModule;\n\n    if (config.baseUrl) {\n      this.client = createOpencodeClient({\n        baseUrl: config.baseUrl,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const serverOptions: {\n      hostname: string;\n      port: number;\n      timeout: number;\n      config?: Record<string, unknown>;\n      env?: Record<string, string>;\n    } = {\n      hostname: config.hostname ?? '127.0.0.1',\n      port: config.port ?? 0,\n      timeout: config.timeout ?? 30000,\n      env: this.buildServerEnv(config),\n    };\n\n    const serverConfig = this.buildServerConfig(config);\n    if (Object.keys(serverConfig).length > 0) {\n      serverOptions.config = serverConfig;\n    }\n\n    const opencode = await createOpencode(serverOptions);\n    this.client = opencode.client;\n    this.server = opencode.server;\n    logger.debug(`OpenCode server started at ${opencode.server.url}`);\n  }\n\n  private async getOrCreateSession(\n    config: OpenCodeSDKConfig,\n    workingDir: string | undefined,\n  ): Promise<OpenCodeSessionContext> {\n    if (!this.client || !this.opencodeModule) {\n      throw new Error('OpenCode SDK client is not initialized');\n    }\n\n    const sessionQuery = this.buildQuery(config, workingDir);\n    if (config.session_id) {\n      return {\n        sessionId: config.session_id,\n        sessionQuery,\n      };\n    }\n\n    const sessionCacheKey = this.buildSessionKey(config, workingDir);\n    if (config.persist_sessions && this.sessions.has(sessionCacheKey)) {\n      return {\n        sessionId: this.sessions.get(sessionCacheKey)!.id,\n        sessionQuery,\n      };\n    }\n\n    const createResult = await this.client.session.create(\n      this.buildCreateSessionParameters(config, sessionQuery),\n    );\n    const createData = unwrapOpenCodeResult(createResult);\n    const sessionId =\n      (createData as { id?: string } | undefined)?.id ??\n      (createResult as { id?: string } | undefined)?.id;\n\n    if (!sessionId) {\n      throw new Error('Failed to get session ID from OpenCode SDK response');\n    }\n\n    const session = {\n      id: sessionId,\n      query: sessionQuery,\n    };\n\n    if (config.persist_sessions) {\n      this.addSession(sessionCacheKey, session);\n      return {\n        sessionId,\n        sessionQuery,\n      };\n    }\n\n    return {\n      sessionId,\n      sessionQuery,\n      ephemeralSession: session,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private buildPromptBody(config: OpenCodeSDKConfig, prompt: string): Record<string, unknown> {\n    if (!this.opencodeModule) {\n      throw new Error('OpenCode SDK module is not loaded');\n    }\n\n    const promptBody: Record<string, unknown> = {\n      parts: [{ type: 'text', text: prompt }],\n    };\n\n    if (config.provider_id || config.model) {\n      promptBody.model = {\n        providerID: config.provider_id ?? '',\n        modelID: config.model ?? '',\n      };\n    }\n\n    const toolsConfig = this.buildToolsConfig(config);\n    if (toolsConfig) {\n      promptBody.tools = toolsConfig;\n    }\n\n    if (config.agent) {\n      promptBody.agent = config.agent;\n    } else if (config.custom_agent) {\n      promptBody.agent = 'custom';\n    }\n\n    if (config.custom_agent?.prompt) {\n      promptBody.system = config.custom_agent.prompt;\n    }\n    if (config.format) {\n      promptBody.format = config.format;\n    }\n    if (config.variant) {\n      promptBody.variant = config.variant;\n    }\n    // v1 accepts permission rules on the prompt payload; v2 moved them to session.create.\n    if (config.permission && this.opencodeModule.apiVersion === 'v1') {\n      promptBody.permission = config.permission;\n    }\n\n    return promptBody;\n  }\n\n  private buildCreateSessionParameters(\n    config: OpenCodeSDKConfig,\n    sessionQuery: OpenCodeSessionQuery | undefined,\n  ): Record<string, unknown> {\n    if (!this.opencodeModule) {\n      throw new Error('OpenCode SDK module is not loaded');\n    }\n\n    const createBody: { title?: string; permission?: OpenCodePermissionConfig } = {\n      title: `promptfoo-${Date.now()}`,\n    };\n    // v2 accepts permission rules when the session is created, not on each prompt.\n    if (config.permission && this.opencodeModule.apiVersion === 'v2') {\n      createBody.permission = config.permission;\n    }\n\n    if (this.opencodeModule.apiVersion === 'v2') {\n      return {\n        ...sessionQuery,\n        ...createBody,\n      };\n    }\n\n    return {\n      body: createBody,\n      query: sessionQuery,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private buildPromptParameters(\n    config: OpenCodeSDKConfig,\n    prompt: string,\n    sessionId: string,\n    sessionQuery: OpenCodeSessionQuery | undefined,\n  ): Record<string, unknown> {\n    if (!this.opencodeModule) {\n      throw new Error('OpenCode SDK module is not loaded');\n    }\n\n    const promptBody = this.buildPromptBody(config, prompt);\n    if (this.opencodeModule.apiVersion === 'v2') {\n      return {\n        sessionID: sessionId,\n        ...sessionQuery,\n        ...promptBody,\n      };\n    }\n\n    return {\n      path: getSessionPath(sessionId),\n      body: promptBody,\n      query: sessionQuery,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private buildProviderResponse(\n    config: OpenCodeSDKConfig,\n    response: OpenCodeSdkResult<OpenCodePromptResponse>,\n    sessionId: string,\n  ): ProviderResponse {\n    const responseData = unwrapOpenCodeResult(response);\n    const assistantMessage = responseData?.info;\n    const parts = responseData?.parts ?? [];\n\n    let output = '';\n    for (const part of parts) {\n      if (part.type === 'text' && part.text) {\n        output += (output ? '\\n' : '') + part.text;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (config.format?.type === 'json_schema') {\n      if (assistantMessage?.structured === undefined) {\n        output = normalizeStructuredText(output) ?? output;\n      } else {\n        output = JSON.stringify(assistantMessage.structured);\n      }\n    }\n\n    const tokens = assistantMessage?.tokens;\n\n    return {\n      output,\n      tokenUsage: tokens\n        ? {\n            prompt: tokens.input ?? 0,\n            completion: tokens.output ?? 0,\n            total: (tokens.input ?? 0) + (tokens.output ?? 0),\n          }\n        : undefined,\n      cost: assistantMessage?.cost ?? 0,\n      raw: JSON.stringify(response),\n      sessionId,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private handleCallError(error: unknown, callOptions?: CallApiOptionsParams): ProviderResponse {\n    const isAbort =\n      (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'AbortError') || callOptions?.abortSignal?.aborted;\n\n    if (isAbort) {\n      logger.warn('OpenCode SDK call aborted');\n      return { error: 'OpenCode SDK call aborted' };\n    }\n\n    if (\n      error &&\n      typeof error === 'object' &&\n      'code' in error &&\n      error.code === 'ENOENT' &&\n      'message' in error &&\n      typeof error.message === 'string' &&\n      error.message.includes('opencode')\n    ) {\n      const cliError = dedent`The OpenCode CLI is required but not installed.\n\n        The OpenCode SDK requires the 'opencode' CLI to be installed and available in your PATH.\n\n        Install it with:\n          curl -fsSL https://opencode.ai/install | bash\n\n        Or see: https://opencode.ai for other installation methods.`;\n      logger.error(cliError);\n      return { error: cliError };\n    }\n\n    const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    logger.error('Error calling OpenCode SDK', { error });\n    return {\n      error: `Error calling OpenCode SDK: ${errorMessage}`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  async callApi(\n    prompt: string,\n    context?: CallApiContextParams,\n    callOptions?: CallApiOptionsParams,\n  ): Promise<ProviderResponse> {\n    const { config, isTempDir, workingDir } = this.prepareCall(context);\n\n    const mcpConfig = config.mcp && Object.keys(config.mcp).length > 0 ? config.mcp : undefined;\n    const cacheResult = await initializeAgenticCache(\n      {\n        cacheKeyPrefix: 'opencode:sdk',\n        workingDir: config.working_dir ? workingDir : undefined,\n        bustCache: context?.bustCache,\n        mcp: mcpConfig,\n        cacheMcp: config.cache_mcp,\n      },\n      {\n        prompt,\n        provider_id: config.provider_id,\n        model: config.model,\n        tools: this.buildToolsConfig(config),\n        permission: config.permission,\n        agent: config.agent,\n        custom_agent: config.custom_agent,\n        workspace: config.workspace,\n        format: config.format,\n        variant: config.variant,\n      },\n    );\n\n    const cachedResponse = await getCachedResponse(cacheResult, 'OpenCode SDK');\n    if (cachedResponse) {\n      return cachedResponse;\n    }\n\n    if (callOptions?.abortSignal?.aborted) {\n      return { error: 'OpenCode SDK call aborted before it started' };\n    }\n\n    let ephemeralSession: OpenCodeSessionHandle | undefined;\n\n    try {\n      await this.ensureClient(config);\n      const session = await this.getOrCreateSession(config, workingDir);\n      ephemeralSession = session.ephemeralSession;\n\n      const promptOptions = this.buildPromptParameters(\n        config,\n        prompt,\n        session.sessionId,\n        session.sessionQuery,\n      );\n\n      logger.debug(`OpenCode SDK prompt options:`, promptOptions);\n\n      const client = this.client;\n      if (!client) {\n        throw new Error('OpenCode SDK client is not initialized');\n      }\n\n      const response = await client.session.prompt(promptOptions);\n      logger.debug(`OpenCode SDK response received`);\n\n      const providerResponse = this.buildProviderResponse(config, response, session.sessionId);\n\n      await cacheResponse(cacheResult, providerResponse, 'OpenCode SDK');\n      logger.debug(`OpenCode SDK response: ${providerResponse.output.slice(0, 100)}...`);\n\n      return providerResponse;\n    } catch (error) {\n      return this.handleCallError(error, callOptions);\n    } finally {\n      if (ephemeralSession) {\n        try {\n          await this.deleteSession(ephemeralSession);\n        } Use offset=561 to continue.)\n</content>\n\n<system-reminder>\nInstructions from: /app/test/AGENTS.md\n# Test Suite\n\n**What this is:** Vitest-based test suite for core promptfoo functionality.\n\n## Running Tests\n\n```bash\n# Run all tests\nnpm test\n\n# Run specific test file\nnpx vitest run providers/openai\n\n# Run tests matching pattern\nnpx vitest run -t \"should handle errors\"\n\n# Run in watch mode\nnpm run test:watch\n\n# Run integration tests\nnpm run test:integration\n```\n\n## Critical Rules\n\n- **NEVER** increase test timeouts - fix the slow test\n- **NEVER** use `.only()` or `.skip()` in committed code\n- **ALWAYS** clean up mocks in `afterEach`\n- Tests run in **random order by default** (configured in vitest.config.ts)\n  - Use `--sequence.shuffle=false` to disable when debugging specific failures\n  - Use `--sequence.seed=12345` to reproduce a specific order\n\n## Writing Tests\n\n**Reference files:**\n\n- **Vitest (frontend)**: `src/app/src/hooks/usePageMeta.test.ts` - explicit imports\n- **Vitest (backend)**: `test/assertions/contains.test.ts` - explicit imports\n\nAll tests require explicit imports from vitest:\n\n```typescript\nimport { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  vi.resetAllMocks(); // Prevents test pollution\n});\n```\n\n## Directory Structure\n\nTests mirror `src/` structure:\n\n- `test/providers/` → `src/providers/`\n- `test/redteam/` → `src/redteam/`\n- `test/agentSkills/` → agent plugin contract tests; read `test/agentSkills/AGENTS.md`\n- `test/fixtures/agent-skills/` → runnable agent skill fixtures; read `test/fixtures/agent-skills/AGENTS.md`\n\n## Mocking\n\n```typescript\nimport { vi } from 'vitest';\n\nvi.mock('axios');\nconst axiosMock = vi.mocked(axios);\naxiosMock.post.mockResolvedValue({ data: { result: 'success' } });\n```\n\n- Use Vitest's mocking utilities (`vi.mock`, `vi.fn`, `vi.spyOn`)\n- Prefer shallow mocking over deep mocking\n- Mock external dependencies but not the code being tested\n- Reset mocks between tests to prevent test pollution\n\n**Critical: Mock Isolation**\n\n`vi.clearAllMocks()` only clears call history, NOT mock implementations. Use `mockReset()` for full isolation:\n\n```typescript\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks(); // Clears .mock.calls and .mock.results\n  vi.mocked(myMock).mockReset(); // Also clears mockReturnValue/mockResolvedValue\n});\n```\n\nFor `vi.hoisted()` mocks or mocks with `mockReturnValue()`, you MUST call `mockReset()` in `beforeEach` to ensure test isolation when tests run in random order.\n\n## Environment Variables\n\nPrefer `mockProcessEnv()` from `test/util/utils.ts` for root tests that need to change environment variables. Use `vi.stubEnv()` only when a test specifically needs Vitest's stub behavior, and pair it with `vi.unstubAllEnvs()`.\n\nAvoid direct `process.env.FOO = ...`, `delete process.env.FOO`, or `process.env = ...` mutations in new tests. The root hygiene suite blocks new files that use direct environment mutation because tests run in random order.\n\n## Zustand Store Testing\n\nWhen testing components that use Zustand stores, prefer **integration testing with real stores** over mocking.\n\n### When to Use Real Stores vs Mocking\n\n**Use real stores (preferred)** when:\n\n- Testing components that modify store state\n- Verifying state changes after user interactions\n- Testing store action logic (merge, update, delete)\n\n**Mock the store** only when:\n\n- Testing pure UI components that only read from stores\n- Testing isolated component logic completely unrelated to state\n\n### Pattern: Integration Testing with Real Stores\n\n**Reference**: `src/app/src/pages/redteam/setup/components/PluginsTab.test.tsx`\n\n```typescript\nimport { act, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';\nimport { beforeEach, afterEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';\nimport { useMyStore } from './useMyStore';\n\n// Capture initial state OUTSIDE describe block\nconst initialState = useMyStore.getState();\n\ndescribe('MyComponent', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    // Reset store to initial state before each test\n    act(() => {\n      useMyStore.setState(initialState);\n    });\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => {\n    // Clean up after each test\n    act(() => {\n      useMyStore.setState(initialState);\n    });\n  });\n\n  test('user interaction updates store correctly', async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n\n    // Verify initial state\n    expect(useMyStore.getState().items).toHaveLength(0);\n\n    render(<MyComponent />);\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add Item' }));\n\n    // Assert on actual store state, not mock calls\n    await waitFor(() => {\n      expect(useMyStore.getState().items).toHaveLength(1);\n    });\n  });\n});\n```\n\n### Key Patterns\n\n1. **Capture initial state outside describe**: `const initialState = useMyStore.getState();`\n2. **Reset in beforeEach AND afterEach**: Ensures test isolation\n3. **Wrap setState in act()**: Required for React state batching\n4. **Assert on getState()**: Verify actual store state, not that a mock was called\n5. **Use waitFor for async**: Store updates may be asynchronous\n6. **Mock external dependencies only**: Mock APIs, analytics, child components—NOT the store\n\n### Anti-Pattern: Mocking the Store (avoid this)\n\n```typescript\n// ❌ AVOID: Mocking the store hook loses integration coverage\nvi.mock('./useMyStore');\nconst mockUpdateItems = vi.fn();\n(useMyStore as any).mockReturnValue({\n  items: [],\n  updateItems: mockUpdateItems,\n});\n\ntest('clicking button calls updateItems', () => {\n  render(<MyComponent />);\n  fireEvent.click(button);\n  // Only verifies mock was called, not that store logic works\n  expect(mockUpdateItems).toHaveBeenCalled();\n});\n```\n\n### Additional Store Test References\n\n- `src/app/src/store/providersStore.test.ts` - Basic store testing\n- `src/app/src/stores/evalConfig.test.ts` - Configuration state\n- `src/app/src/stores/userStore.test.ts` - Async operations with act()\n\n## Provider Testing\n\nEvery provider needs tests covering:\n\n1. Success case\n2. Error cases (4xx, 5xx, rate limits)\n3. Configuration validation\n4. Token usage tracking\n\nWhen a change introduces request-format gating, parser selection, or middleware validation, add negative-path tests for malformed inputs and assert the exact response contract.\n\nSee `test/providers/openai-codex-sdk.test.ts` for reference patterns.\n\n## Smoke Tests\n\nSmoke tests verify the **built CLI package** works correctly end-to-end. They test `dist/src/main.js` directly using `spawnSync`.\n\n```bash\n# Run smoke tests\nnpm run test:smoke\n\n# Run specific smoke test file\nnpx vitest run test/smoke/cli.test.ts --config vitest.smoke.config.ts\n```\n\n**Location:** `test/smoke/` with fixtures in `test/smoke/fixtures/configs/`\n\n**Reference pattern** (from `test/smoke/filters-and-flags.test.ts`):\n\n```typescript\nimport { spawnSync } from 'child_process';\nimport * as fs from 'fs';\nimport * as path from 'path';\nimport { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';\n\nconst CLI_PATH = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../dist/src/main.js');\nconst FIXTURES_DIR = path.resolve(__dirname, 'fixtures/configs');\nconst OUTPUT_DIR = path.resolve(__dirname, '.temp-output');\n\nfunction runCli(args: string[], options: { cwd?: string } = {}) {\n  const result = spawnSync('node', [CLI_PATH, ...args], {\n    cwd: options.cwd || path.resolve(__dirname, '../..'),\n    encoding: 'utf-8',\n    env: { ...process.env, NO_COLOR: '1' },\n    timeout: 60000,\n  });\n  return {\n    stdout: result.stdout || '',\n    stderr: result.stderr || '',\n    exitCode: result.status ?? 1,\n  };\n}\n\ndescribe('My Smoke Tests', () => {\n  beforeAll(() => {\n    if (!fs.existsSync(CLI_PATH)) {\n      throw new Error(`Built CLI not found. Run 'npm run build' first.`);\n    }\n    fs.mkdirSync(OUTPUT_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  });\n\n  afterAll(() => {\n    fs.rmSync(OUTPUT_DIR, { recursive: true, force: true });\n  });\n\n  it('runs eval with echo provider', () => {\n    const configPath = path.join(FIXTURES_DIR, 'basic.yaml');\n    const outputPath = path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, 'output.json');\n\n    const { exitCode } = runCli(['eval', '-c', configPath, '-o', outputPath, '--no-cache']);\n\n    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(outputPath, 'utf-8'));\n    expect(parsed.results.results[0].response.output).toContain('Hello');\n  });\n});\n```\n\n**Key patterns:**\n\n- Test the **built package** (`dist/src/main.js`), not source code\n- Use `spawnSync` with `node` to run the CLI directly\n- Fixtures go in `test/smoke/fixtures/configs/` (committed to git)\n- Temp output directories cleaned up in `afterAll`\n- Use `echo` provider for deterministic, zero-cost testing\n- Check exit codes AND output file contents\n\n**Fixture example** (`test/smoke/fixtures/configs/basic.yaml`):\n\n```yaml\nproviders:\n  - echo\n\nprompts:\n  - 'Hello {{name}}'\n\ntests:\n  - vars:\n      name: World\n    assert:\n      - type: contains\n        value: World\n```\n\nSee `docs/plans/smoke-tests.md` for the full test checklist.\n\n## Test Configuration\n\n- Config: `vitest.config.ts` (unit tests), `vitest.integration.config.ts` (integration tests), `vitest.smoke.config.ts` (smoke tests)\n- Setup: `vitest.setup.ts`\n- Globals disabled: All test utilities must be explicitly imported from `vitest`\n- Import `describe`, `it`, `expect`, `beforeEach`, `afterEach`, `vi` from `vitest`\n\n## Testing Code with Timers\n\nWhen testing code that uses `setTimeout`, `setInterval`, or `Date.now()`, use **fake timers** to make tests deterministic and fast.\n\n**Reference files:**\n\n- `test/scheduler/slotQueue.test.ts` - Comprehensive fake timer usage\n- `test/scheduler/providerRateLimitState.test.ts` - Async code with timers\n\n### Basic Pattern\n\n```typescript\nimport { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\ndescribe('MyTimerCode', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    vi.useFakeTimers();\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => {\n    vi.useRealTimers();\n  });\n\n  it('handles timeout', () => {\n    const callback = vi.fn();\n    setTimeout(callback, 1000);\n\n    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000);\n\n    expect(callback).toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n});\n```\n\n### Async Code with Timers\n\nWhen testing async functions that internally use timers, you cannot simply `await` them—the promise will hang because fake timers don't advance automatically. Use this pattern:\n\n```typescript\nit('handles async retry with delay', async () => {\n  // 1. Start the promise (don't await yet)\n  const promise = myAsyncFunctionWithRetry();\n\n  // 2. Run all timers to completion\n  await vi.runAllTimersAsync();\n\n  // 3. Now await the result\n  const result = await promise;\n\n  expect(result).toBe('success');\n});\n```\n\n### Why Fake Timers Matter\n\nReal timers cause flaky tests because:\n\n- **Timer resolution varies**: Windows has ~15ms minimum resolution vs ~1-4ms on Linux/Mac\n- **Race conditions**: Code checking `Date.now()` multiple times can see different values if a millisecond boundary is crossed\n- **CI variability**: Tests may pass locally but fail under CI load\n\n**Anti-pattern** (causes flaky tests):\n\n```typescript\n// ❌ AVOID: Real timers with small delays\nit('retries after delay', async () => {\n  const result = await functionThatRetries({ retryAfterMs: 1 }); // May timeout randomly\n});\n```\n\n**Correct pattern**:\n\n```typescript\n// ✓ CORRECT: Fake timers with controlled advancement\nit('retries after delay', async () => {\n  vi.useFakeTimers();\n  const promise = functionThatRetries({ retryAfterMs: 1000 });\n  await vi.runAllTimersAsync();\n  const result = await promise;\n  vi.useRealTimers();\n});\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n- Ensure all tests are independent and can run in any order\n- Clean up any test data or mocks after each test\n- Run the full test suite before committing changes\n- Test failures should be deterministic\n- For database tests, use in-memory instances or proper test fixtures\n- **Use fake timers** for any code involving `setTimeout`, `setInterval`, or timing-sensitive logic\n\n</system-reminder>","metadata":{"preview":"import fs from 'fs';\n\nimport { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from
